      "tel": "+19876543210",
      "name": "Bob"
    }

B.2. Email Thread Multipart vCon

The following is an unsigned form of an vCon for a 2 message email thread between 2 parties. The email messages are multipart MIME message bodies.¶

The unformatted version of the following example can be downloaded from: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/ietf-wg-vcon/draft-ietf-vcon-vcon-core/refs/heads/main/examples/ab_email_acct_prob_thread.vcon¶

Note to the RFC Editor: This example, and its unformatted version at the URL above, contain the vcon parameter (see Section 4.1.1), which is included in the Internet-Draft versions of this document to indicate the version of the schema upon which the example is based. The vcon parameter is to be removed from the example and its unformatted version before publication as an RFC. Please remove this note before publication.¶

B.3. Email Thread Text vCon

The following is an unsigned form of an vCon for a 3 message email thread between 2 parties. The email messages are plain text message bodies.¶

The unformatted version of the following example can be downloaded from: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/ietf-wg-vcon/draft-ietf-vcon-vcon-core/refs/heads/main/examples/ab_email_prob_followup_text_thread.vcon¶

Note to the RFC Editor: This example, and its unformatted version at the URL above, contain the vcon parameter (see Section 4.1.1), which is included in the Internet-Draft versions of this document to indicate the version of the schema upon which the example is based. The vcon parameter is to be removed from the example and its unformatted version before publication as an RFC. Please remove this note before publication.¶

{
  "vcon": "0.5.0",
  "parties": [
    {
      "mailto": "a@example.com",
      "name": "Alice"
    },
    {
      "mailto": "b@example.com",
      "name": "Bob"
    }
  ],
  "dialog": [
    {
      "type": "text",
      "start": "2022-09-23T23:24:59.000+00:00",
      "duration": 0,
      "parties": [
        0
      ],
      "mediatype": "text/plain",
      "encoding": "none",
      "body": "Hi Bob:\nI just wanted to follow up to see that yo
        ur account is ok now.\n\nRegards,\nAlice\n\n",
      "message_id": "<1402576211.401234.1663975499936@mail.yahoo.
        com>"
    },
    {
      "type": "text",
      "start": "2022-09-23T23:34:32.000+00:00",
      "duration": 0,
      "parties": [
        1
      ],
      "mediatype": "text/plain; charset=UTF-8",
      "encoding": "none",
      "body": "Hi Alice:\nAll is fine. Thanks.\n\n    On Friday,
        September 23, 2022, 23:24:59 PM UTC, Alice <a@example.com
        > wrote:  \n \n  Hi Bob:\n  I just wanted to follow up to
         see that your account is ok now.\n  \n  Regards,\n  Alic
        e\n",
      "message_id": "<1879233410.402112.1663976072118@mail.yahoo.
        com>"
    },
    {
      "type": "text",
      "start": "2022-09-23T23:38:12.000+00:00",
      "duration": 0,
      "parties": [
        0
      ],
      "mediatype": "text/plain",
      "encoding": "none",
      "body": "Awesome!\n\n    On Friday, September 23, 2022, 23:
        34: PM UTC, Bob <b@example.com> wrote:  \n\n  Hi Alice:\n
          All is fine. Thanks.\n  \n      On Friday, September 23
        , 2022, 23:24:59 PM UTC, Alice <a@example.com> wrote:  \n

        e\n",

B.4. Two Party Call vCon With Externally Referenced Recording

This example vCon is for a simple 2 party PSTN call. It has a single Dialog Object which reference a single channel wav format recording with the two parties audio mixed into the single channel.¶

The unformatted version of the following example can be downloaded from: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/ietf-wg-vcon/draft-ietf-vcon-vcon-core/refs/heads/main/examples/ab_call_ext_rec.vcon¶

Note to the RFC Editor: This example, and its unformatted version at the URL above, contain the vcon parameter (see Section 4.1.1), which is included in the Internet-Draft versions of this document to indicate the version of the schema upon which the example is based. The vcon parameter is to be removed from the example and its unformatted version before publication as an RFC. Please remove this note before publication.¶

¶

B.6. Signed vCon

This example vCon is the signed form of the Two Party Call vCon With Externally Referenced Recording (Appendix B.4) example. The private key used to sign this can be found at:¶

https://raw.githubusercontent.com/vcon-dev/vcon/main/certs/fake_grp.key
¶

The certificate chain is included in the x5c parameter of the header Object.¶

The unformatted version of the following example can be downloaded from: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/ietf-wg-vcon/draft-ietf-vcon-vcon-core/refs/heads/main/examples/ab_call_ext_rec_signed.vcon¶

Note to the RFC Editor: This example, and its unformatted version at the URL above, contain the vcon parameter (see Section 4.1.1), which is included in the Internet-Draft versions of this document to indicate the version of the schema upon which the example is based. The vcon parameter is to be removed from the example and its unformatted version before publication as an RFC. Please remove this note before publication.¶

B.7. Encrypted vCon

This example vCon is the encrypted form of the Signed vCon (Appendix B.6) example. The private key to decrypt it can be found at:¶

https://raw.githubusercontent.com/vcon-dev/vcon/main/certs/fake_grp.key
¶

The unformatted version of the following example can be downloaded from: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/ietf-wg-vcon/draft-ietf-vcon-vcon-core/refs/heads/main/examples/ab_call_ext_rec_encrypted.vcon¶

Note to the RFC Editor: This example, and its unformatted version at the URL above, contain the vcon parameter (see Section 4.1.1), which is included in the Internet-Draft versions of this document to indicate the version of the schema upon which the example is based. The vcon parameter is to be removed from the example and its unformatted version before publication as an RFC. Please remove this note before publication.¶

B.8. Redacted vCon

This is an example redaction of the vCon in the example Two Party Call vCon With Externally Referenced Recording (Appendix B.4). Most notable is the Redacted_from object (Section 4.1.8) which references the lesser redacted version of this vCon. In addition the url parameter has been redacted from the Dialog Object (Section 4.5), but the rest of the Dialog Object (Section 4.5) was left in the redaction.¶

The unformatted version of the following example can be downloaded from: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/ietf-wg-vcon/draft-ietf-vcon-vcon-core/refs/heads/main/examples/ab_call_ext_rec_redacted.vcon¶

Note to the RFC Editor: This example, and its unformatted version at the URL above, contain the vcon parameter (see Section 4.1.1), which is included in the Internet-Draft versions of this document to indicate the version of the schema upon which the example is based. The vcon parameter is to be removed from the example and its unformatted version before publication as an RFC. Please remove this note before publication.¶

B.9. Amended Signed vCon

This is an example vCon with an Amended_from Object (Section 4.1.9.1) referencing the vCon that it amends or adds additional content. In this case it is referencing the signed vCon example above. An example text Dialog Object (index 1) is the amendment.¶

The unformatted version of the following example can be downloaded from: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/ietf-wg-vcon/draft-ietf-vcon-vcon-core/refs/heads/main/examples/ab_call_ext_rec_amended.vcon¶

Note to the RFC Editor: This example, and its unformatted version at the URL above, contain the vcon parameter (see Section 4.1.1), which is included in the Internet-Draft versions of this document to indicate the version of the schema upon which the example is based. The vcon parameter is to be removed from the example and its unformatted version before publication as an RFC. Please remove this note before publication.¶

Appendix C. vCon JSON Schema

This JSON schema is provided as informational. The text in sections 1-6 of this document define the normative definition of the vCon schema. If the JSON schema provided below differs from that in the text of the above sections, the text should be taken as the definitive definition and the JSON Schema below should be consider incorrect. The JSON Schema is permissive: parameters and type values which are not defined in this document do not cause validation to fail.¶

The JSON Schema definition below has been formatted with line breaks to print nicely on the page. The unformatted version of the JSON schema can be downloaded from: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/ietf-wg-vcon/draft-ietf-vcon-vcon-core/refs/heads/main/vcon_json_schema.json¶

{
  "$schema": "http://json-schema.org/draft-07/schema#",
  "$id": "https://ietf.org/vcon/schemas/unsigned-vcon.json",
  "title": "vCon - Unsigned Form",
  "description": "JSON schema for the unsigned form of vCon (Conv
    ersational Data Container) as defined in RFCXXXX",
  "type": "object",
  "required": [
    "uuid",
    "created_at"
  ],
  "properties": {
    "vcon": {
      "type": "string",
      "description": "DEPRECATED: Syntactic version of the JSON f
        ormat. This was used to indicate schema changes in the In
        ternet-Draft versions",
      "const": "0.5.0"
    },
    "uuid": {
      "type": "string",
      "description": "Globally unique identifier for the vCon. SH
        OULD be a version 8 UUID",
      "format": "uuid"
    },
    "extensions": {
      "type": "array",
      "description": "List of names of all vCon extensions used b
        y this vCon",
      "items": {
        "type": "string"
      }
    },
    "critical": {
      "type": "array",
      "description": "List of extension names that a consumer MUS
        T support to safely process this vCon",
      "items": {
        "type": "string"
      }
    },
    "created_at": {
      "type": "string",
      "description": "Creation time of this vCon in RFC3339 forma
        t",
      "format": "date-time"
    },
    "updated_at": {
      "type": "string",
      "description": "Last modified time of this vCon in RFC3339
        format",
      "format": "date-time"
    },
    "subject": {
      "type": "string",
      "description": "Subject or topic of the conversation"
    },
    "redacted": {
      "type": "object",
      "description": "Reference to the unredacted or less redacte
        d vCon prior instance",
      "required": [
        "type"
      ],
      "properties": {
        "uuid": {
          "type": "string",
          "description": "UUID of the unredacted or less redacted
             vCon prior instance",
          "format": "uuid"
        },
        "type": {
          "type": "string",
          "description": "Type of redaction performed"
        },
        "url": {
          "type": "string",
          "description": "HTTPS URL where the referenced vCon is
            stored",
          "format": "uri"
        },
        "content_hash": {
          "oneOf": [
            {
              "type": "string"
            },
            {
              "type": "array",
              "items": {
                "type": "string"
              }
            }
          ],
          "description": "Hash(es) of the external content using
            format: algorithm-base64url_encoded_hash"
        }
      },
      "dependencies": {
        "url": [
          "content_hash"
        ]
      }
    },
    "amended": {
      "type": "object",
      "description": "Reference to the prior vCon instance versio
        n that this vCon amends",
      "properties": {
        "uuid": {
          "type": "string",
          "description": "UUID of the prior vCon instance version
            ",
          "format": "uuid"
        },
        "url": {
          "type": "string",
          "description": "HTTPS URL where the referenced vCon is
            stored",
          "format": "uri"
        },
        "content_hash": {
          "oneOf": [
            {
              "type": "string"
            },
            {
              "type": "array",
              "items": {
                "type": "string"
              }
            }
          ],
          "description": "Hash(es) of the external content (requi
            red if url is provided)"
        }
      },
      "dependencies": {
        "url": [
          "content_hash"
        ]
      },
      "if": {
        "not": {
          "required": [
            "url"
          ]
        }
      },
      "then": {
        "required": [
          "uuid"
        ],
        "$comment": "uuid is optional only if an external referen
          ce is provided"
      }
    },
    "sessions": {
      "type": "array",
      "description": "Array of Session Objects, each grouping the
         Dialog and Event Objects of one session of the conversat
        ion",
      "items": {
        "$ref": "#/definitions/Session"
      }
    },
    "parties": {
      "type": "array",
      "description": "Array of Party Objects representing all par
        ties involved in the conversation",
      "items": {
        "$ref": "#/definitions/Party"
      }
    },
    "events": {
      "type": "array",
      "description": "Array of Event Objects; order is not signif
        icant, Event Objects are sequenced by their time paramete
        r",
      "items": {
        "$ref": "#/definitions/Event"
      }
    },
    "dialog": {
      "type": "array",
      "description": "Array of Dialog Objects containing the capt
        ured conversation content",
      "items": {
        "$ref": "#/definitions/Dialog"
      }
    },
    "analysis": {
      "type": "array",
      "description": "Array of Analysis Objects containing analys
        is performed on the conversational data",
      "items": {
        "$ref": "#/definitions/Analysis"
      }
    },
    "attachments": {
      "type": "array",
      "description": "Array of Attachment Objects for ancillary d
        ocuments related to the conversation",
      "items": {
        "$ref": "#/definitions/Attachment"
      }
    }
  },
  "definitions": {
    "Session": {
      "type": "object",
      "description": "Represents one session of the conversation
        and references the Dialog Objects which are part of it; a
         Session Object with no parameters is permitted",
      "properties": {
        "start": {
          "type": "string",
          "format": "date-time",
          "description": "Start time of the session in RFC3339 fo
            rmat; SHOULD be present"
        },
        "duration": {
          "anyOf": [
            {
              "type": "integer",
              "minimum": 0
            },
            {
              "type": "number",
              "minimum": 0
            }
          ],
          "description": "Duration of the session in seconds"
        },
        "parties": {
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "type": "integer",
            "minimum": 0
          },
          "description": "Indices of all parties that were part o
            f the session; SHOULD be present; the first party is
            the implied originator"
        },
        "originator": {
          "type": "integer",
          "minimum": 0,
          "description": "Index of the originating party; only pr
            ovided if the first party in parties is not the origi
            nator"
        },
        "session_id": {
          "$ref": "#/definitions/SessionId"
        },
        "sessions": {
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "$ref": "#/definitions/SessionReference"
          },
          "description": "Relationships to other Session Objects"
        },
        "dialog": {
          "type": "array",
          "items": {
            "type": "integer",
            "minimum": 0
          },
          "description": "Indices of the Dialog Objects which are
             part of the session"
        }
      }
    },
    "SessionReference": {
      "type": "object",
      "description": "Session_Reference Object: the relationship
        of another Session Object to the Session Object which con
        tains this reference",
      "required": [
        "session",
        "type"
      ],
      "properties": {
        "session": {
          "type": "integer",
          "minimum": 0,
          "description": "Index of the referenced Session Object
            in the sessions array"
        },
        "type": {
          "type": "string",
          "description": "Type of relationship: child, peer, prec
            ursor, breakout, or a value defined in a vCon extensi
            on"
        },
        "description": {
          "type": "string",
          "description": "Free form text description of the relat
            ionship"
        }
      }
    },
    "Party": {
      "type": "object",
      "description": "Represents a party involved in the conversa
        tion",
      "properties": {
        "tel": {
          "type": "string",
          "description": "TEL URL (RFC3966) for the party"
        },
        "sip": {
          "type": "string",
          "description": "SIP URL for the party"
        },
        "stir": {
          "type": "string",
          "description": "STIR PASSporT in JWS Compact Serializat
            ion form"
        },
        "mailto": {
          "type": "string",
          "description": "Email address for the party in any comm
            on form, including a bare address or a MAILTO URL"
        },
        "name": {
          "type": "string",
          "description": "Name of the party"
        },
        "did": {
          "type": "string",
          "description": "Decentralized Identifier (DID) URI for
            the party"
        },
        "validation": {
          "type": "string",
          "description": "Label or token identifying the method o
            f identity validation used"
        },
        "gmlpos": {
          "type": "string",
          "description": "Geographic location in GML pos format (
            latitude longitude)"
        },
        "civicaddress": {
          "$ref": "#/definitions/Civicaddress"
        },
        "uuid": {
          "type": "string",
          "description": "Free form unique identifier for the par
            ticipant; not constrained to UUID syntax"
        },
        "type": {
          "type": "string",
          "description": "Participant type"
        },
        "org": {
          "type": "string",
          "description": "Organization to which the party belongs
            "
        },
        "dept": {
          "type": "string",
          "description": "Department to which the party belongs"
        }
      }
    },
    "Civicaddress": {
      "type": "object",
      "description": "Civic address information for a party's loc
        ation",
      "properties": {
        "country": {
          "type": "string"
        },
        "a1": {
          "type": "string",

        },
        "a2": {

        },
        "a3": {

          "description": "City/township"
        },
        "a4": {

        },
        "a5": {

        },
        "a6": {
          "type": "string",

        },
        "prd": {

        },
        "pod": {

        },
        "sts": {
          "type": "string",
          "description": "Street suffix"
        },
        "hno": {
          "type": "string",
          "description": "House number"
        },
        "hns": {

          "description": "House number suffix"
        },
        "lmk": {

        },

          "description": "Floor"

          "type": "string",
          "description": "Name/occupant"
        },
        "pc": {
          "type": "string",
          "description": "Postal code"
        }
      }
    },
    "Event": {
      "type": "object",
      "description": "Represents an event which occurred in a ses
        sion, such as a party joining, a key press or a transfer"
        ,
      "required": [
        "type",
        "time",
        "session",
        "party"
      ],
      "properties": {
        "type": {
          "type": "string",
          "description": "Type of event: join, drop, hold, unhold
            , mute, unmute, keydown, keyup, consultation-start, b
            lind-transfer, consultative-transfer, transfer-abando
            ned, or a value defined in a vCon extension"
        },
        "time": {
          "type": "string",
          "format": "date-time",
          "description": "Time of the event in RFC3339 format"
        },
        "session": {
          "type": "integer",
          "minimum": 0,
          "description": "Index of the Session Object in which th
            e event occurred; for transfer event types, the Sessi
            on Object for the original call"
        },
        "party": {
          "type": "integer",
          "minimum": 0,
          "description": "Index of the party to which the event a
            pplies; for transfer event types, the Transferor"
        },
        "dialog": {
          "type": "integer",
          "minimum": 0,
          "description": "Index of the Dialog Object associated w
            ith the event; MUST NOT be present for transfer event
             types"
        },
        "button": {
          "type": "string",
          "description": "DTMF digit, character or string (requir
            ed for keydown and keyup events)"
        },
        "transferee": {
          "type": "integer",
          "minimum": 0,
          "description": "Party index of the Transferee (transfer
             event types)"
        },
        "transfer_target": {
          "type": "integer",
          "minimum": 0,
          "description": "Party index of the Transfer Target (tra
            nsfer event types; optional for transfer-abandoned)"
        },
        "consultation": {
          "type": "integer",
          "minimum": 0,
          "description": "Session index of the consultative call
            (consultation-start and consultative-transfer; option
            al for transfer-abandoned)"
        },
        "target_session": {
          "type": "integer",
          "minimum": 0,
          "description": "Session index of the target call (blind
            -transfer and consultative-transfer)"
        }
      },
      "allOf": [
        {
          "if": {
            "properties": {
              "type": {
                "enum": [
                  "join",
                  "drop",
                  "hold",
                  "unhold",
                  "mute",

¶

Appendix D. vCon CDDL

This CDDL for vCon core is provided as informational. The text in sections 1-6 of this document define the normative definition of the vCon CDDL. If the CDDL provided below differs or conflicts from that in the text of the above sections, the text should be taken as the definitive definition and the CDDL below should be consider incorrect. The CDDL is strict: parameters and type values which are not defined in this document cause validation to fail unless the CDDL for the vCon extension which defines them is also used. Failing validation against this CDDL is not by itself a reason to reject a vCon (see Section 2.5).¶

; CDDL for the JSON vCon - Conversation Data Container
; Generated from the prose of draft-ietf-vcon-vcon-core
; (syntax version 0.5.0).
; The prose of the draft is normative.
; This CDDL is informative.
; Constraints that cannot be expressed in CDDL are stated in
; comments.
;
; This CDDL follows the text of the draft at commit 25c1397.
;
; This CDDL is strict.  A parameter or value which is not defined
; here does not validate, unless the CDDL of the vCon extension
; which defines it is used together with this CDDL.  Failing
; validation against this CDDL is not by itself a reason to
; reject a vCon.  The draft defines how parameters and values
; which are not recognized are handled (see Extending vCon).
;
; Extension points
; ----------------
; The extension points are CDDL sockets (RFC 8610, Section 3.9).
;
; 1. Adding parameters to an Object.
;
; Each Object ends with a group socket named for that Object.
; A vCon extension adds parameters to that Object only, with for
; example:
;
;     $$party-ext //= ( ? example_parameter: tstr )
;
; The "*" before each of these sockets allows the socket to match
; nothing, so that this CDDL can be used with no extensions, and
; allows more than one extension to add to the same Object.
;
; 2. Adding a value which selects other parameters.
;
; The dialog-type-ext socket adds a Dialog Object type along with
; the parameters which apply to that type.  The event-type-ext
; socket does the same for an Event Object type.  The encoding-ext
; socket adds an encoding value along with the form of the body
; parameter for that encoding.  For example:
;
;     $$dialog-type-ext //= example_dialog_type
;     example_dialog_type = (
;         type: "example",
;         dialog_common_type,
;         ? example_parameter: tstr,
;         * $$example-dialog-ext
;       )
;
;     $$encoding-ext //= ( encoding: "example", body: tstr )
;
; A group which defines a new type is best given a rule name and
; ended with a socket of its own, as above, so that another
; extension can add parameters to that type.
;
; 3. Adding a value to a list of values.
;
; The disposition-ext socket adds a disposition value.  The
; session-reference-type-ext socket adds a Session_Reference
; Object type value.  For example:
;
;     $disposition-ext /= "example"
;
; The sockets in 2 and 3 are alternatives in a choice.  They have
; no "*" and are not to be given one.
;
; The header objects of the signed and encrypted forms have no
; socket.  They are open, as any JWS or JWE header parameter MAY
; be present in them.

; A vCon JSON object is in one of three forms: unsigned, signed
; (JWS) or encrypted (JWE).

vcon = unsigned_vcon_type / signed_vcon_type / encrypted_vcon_type

; Unsigned Form of vCon Object
;_____________________________

; Not enforced by this CDDL:
; - At least one Session Object MUST exist if the events array or
;   the dialog array contain Objects.
; - An index MUST refer to an Object which exists in the array
;   that it indexes.
unsigned_vcon_type = {
    ; DEPRECATED
    ? "vcon": "0.5.0",
    uuid: uuid_value_type,
    ? extensions: [* tstr],
    ? critical: [* tstr],
    created_at: date_type,
    ? updated_at: date_type,
    ? subject: tstr,
    ; redacted and amended are mutually exclusive
    ? ( redacted: redacted_object_type //
        amended: amended_object_type ),
    ; at least one of parties, dialog, analysis or attachments
    ; SHOULD be present
    ? sessions: [* session_object_type],
    ? parties: [* party_object_type],
    ? events: [* event_object_type],
    ? dialog: [* dialog_object_type],
    ? analysis: [* analysis_object_type],
    ? attachments: [* attachment_object_type],
    * $$vcon-ext
  }

; Object and multi-parameter types
;_________________________________

; Redacted Object
; uuid SHOULD be provided.
; content_hash MUST be provided if url is provided.
redacted_object_type = {
    ? uuid: uuid_value_type,
    type: tstr,
    ? ( url: url_value_type,
        content_hash: content_hash_type ),
    * $$redacted-ext
  }

; Amended Object
; uuid is optional only if an external reference is provided.
; content_hash MUST be provided if url is provided.
amended_object_type = {
    ? uuid: uuid_value_type,
    ? ( url: url_value_type,
        content_hash: content_hash_type ),
    * $$amended-ext
  }

; Session Object
; A Session Object with no parameters is permitted.
; start SHOULD be equal to or before the start of all of the
; Dialog Objects and the time of all of the Event Objects which
; are part of the session.  This is not enforced by this CDDL.
session_object_type = {
    ; start SHOULD be present
    ? start: date_type,
    ? duration: duration_type,
    ; parties SHOULD be present; the first party is the implied
    ; originator of the session
    ? parties: [* party_index_type],
    ; only provided if the first party in parties is not the
    ; originator
    ? originator: party_index_type,
    ; a single SessionId Object
    ? session_id: session_id_object_type,
    ? sessions: [* session_reference_object_type],
    ? dialog: [* dialog_index_type],
    * $$session-ext
  }

; Session_Reference Object
session_reference_object_type = {
    session: session_index_type,
    type: session_reference_type_value_type,
    ? description: tstr,
    * $$session-reference-ext
  }

session_reference_type_value_type = "child" / "peer" /
    "precursor" / "breakout" / $session-reference-type-ext

; Event Object
; The type selects which parameters apply (see Event Object
; Parameter Applicability by Type).
; A vCon extension adds an Event Object type through the
; event-type-ext socket and adds parameters to all Event Object
; types through the event-ext socket.
event_object_type = {
    ( event_party_type //
      event_key_type //
      event_consultation_start_type //
      event_blind_transfer_type //
      event_consultative_transfer_type //
      event_transfer_abandoned_type //
      $$event-type-ext ),
    * $$event-ext
  }

; Parameters common to all Event Object types
; In the transfer event types, session references the Session
; Object for the original call and party references the
; Transferor.
event_common_type = (
    "time": date_type,
    session: session_index_type,
    party: party_index_type
  )

; Party event types
event_party_type = (
    type: "join" / "drop" / "hold" / "unhold" / "mute" / "unmute",
    event_common_type,
    ? dialog: dialog_index_type
  )

; Key event types
event_key_type = (
    type: "keydown" / "keyup",
    event_common_type,
    ? dialog: dialog_index_type,
    button: tstr
  )

; Transfer event types
; transferee and transfer_target reference Party Objects.
; consultation and target_session reference Session Objects.
event_consultation_start_type = (
    type: "consultation-start",
    event_common_type,
    transferee: party_index_type,
    transfer_target: party_index_type,
    consultation: session_index_type
  )

event_blind_transfer_type = (
    type: "blind-transfer",
    event_common_type,
    transferee: party_index_type,
    transfer_target: party_index_type,
    target_session: session_index_type
  )

event_consultative_transfer_type = (
    type: "consultative-transfer",
    event_common_type,
    transferee: party_index_type,
    transfer_target: party_index_type,
    consultation: session_index_type,
    target_session: session_index_type
  )

event_transfer_abandoned_type = (
    type: "transfer-abandoned",
    event_common_type,
    transferee: party_index_type,
    ? transfer_target: party_index_type,
    ? consultation: session_index_type
  )

; Party Object
; A Party Object with no parameters is permitted.
party_object_type = {
    ? tel: tstr,
    ? sip: tstr,
    ? stir: tstr,
    ? mailto: tstr,
    ? name: tstr,
    ? did: tstr,
    ? validation: tstr,
    ? gmlpos: tstr,
    ? civicaddress: civicaddress_type,
    ; a free form unique identifier for the participant;
    ; not constrained to UUID syntax
    ? uuid: tstr,
    ; type SHOULD be "person", "bot" or "organization"
    ? type: tstr,
    ? org: tstr,
    ? dept: tstr,
    * $$party-ext
  }

; Civicaddress Object
civicaddress_type = {
    ? country: tstr,
    ? a1: tstr,
    ? a2: tstr,
    ? a3: tstr,
    ? a4: tstr,
    ? a5: tstr,
    ? a6: tstr,
    ? prd: tstr,
    ? pod: tstr,
    ? sts: tstr,
    ? hno: tstr,
    ? hns: tstr,
    ? lmk: tstr,
    ? loc: tstr,
    ? flr: tstr,
    ? nam: tstr,
    ? pc: tstr,
    * $$civicaddress-ext
  }

; Dialog Object
; The type selects which parameters apply (see Dialog Object
; Parameter Applicability by Type).
; A placeholder Dialog Object contains only the type parameter and,
; for the "incomplete" type, the required disposition parameter.
; A placeholder Dialog Object MUST be of type "recording" if the
; call was set up, or of type "incomplete" if it was not.
; A vCon extension adds a Dialog Object type through the
; dialog-type-ext socket and adds parameters to all Dialog Object
; types through the dialog-ext socket.
dialog_object_type = {
    ( dialog_recording_type //
      dialog_text_type //
      dialog_incomplete_type //
      $$dialog-type-ext ),
    * $$dialog-ext
  }

; Parameters common to all Dialog Object types
dialog_common_type = (
    ; start SHOULD be present; MAY be omitted if not known
    ? start: date_type,
    ? duration: duration_type,
    ? session_id: session_id_parameter_type,
    ? application: tstr
  )

dialog_recording_type = (
    type: "recording",
    dialog_common_type,
    dialog_media_based_type
  )

dialog_text_type = (
    type: "text",
    dialog_common_type,
    dialog_media_based_type
  )

; Parameters of the "recording" and "text" type Dialog Objects
dialog_media_based_type = (
    ; parties SHOULD be present
    ? parties: dialog_parties_type,
    ; mediatype MUST be present for inline content; not required
    ; when Dialog Content is absent
    ? mediatype: media_type_value_type,
    ? filename: tstr,
    ? message_id: tstr,
    ; disposition SHOULD NOT be present
    ? disposition: disposition_value_type,
    ; Dialog Content SHOULD be present;
    ; body or url MAY be absent if redacted
    ? file_content_type
  )

; mediatype, filename, message_id and Dialog Content MUST NOT be
; present in "incomplete" type Dialog Objects.
dialog_incomplete_type = (
    type: "incomplete",
    dialog_common_type,
    ? parties: dialog_parties_type,
    ; "failed" SHOULD be used when the reason is not known
    disposition: disposition_value_type
  )

; Attachment Object
attachment_object_type = {
    ? purpose: tstr,
    start: date_type,
    party: party_index_type,
    ; at least one of dialog or session MUST be present
    ( ( dialog: dialog_index_type,
        ? session: session_index_or_list_type ) //
      session: session_index_or_list_type ),
    ; mediatype MUST be present for inline content; not required
    ; when Attachment Content is absent
    ? mediatype: media_type_value_type,
    ? filename: tstr,
    ; Attachment Content SHOULD be present;
    ; body or url MAY be absent if redacted
    ? file_content_type,
    * $$attachment-ext
  }

; Analysis Object
; At least one of dialog, session, attachment or analysis SHOULD
; be present.
; An Analysis Object MUST NOT reference itself in the analysis
; parameter.  This is not enforced by this CDDL.
analysis_object_type = {
    ; type SHOULD be one of: "report", "sentiment", "summary",
    ; "transcript", "translation", "tts"
    type: tstr,
    ? dialog: dialog_index_or_list_type,
    ? session: session_index_or_list_type,
    ? attachment: attachment_index_or_list_type,
    ? analysis: analysis_index_or_list_type,
    ; mediatype SHOULD be present for inline content; when no
    ; media type is defined for the analysis data format, the
    ; vendor, product and schema parameters SHOULD identify the
    ; format instead
    ? mediatype: media_type_value_type,
    ? filename: tstr,
    vendor: tstr,
    ? product: tstr,
    ? schema: tstr,
    ; Analysis Content SHOULD be present;
    ; body or url MAY be absent if redacted
    ? file_content_type,
    * $$analysis-ext
  }

; Inline Files and Externally Referenced Files
;_____________________________________________

file_content_type = ( inline_content_type // external_content_type )

; body is any JSON value when encoding is "json";
; otherwise body is a string.
; A vCon extension which adds an encoding value also defines the
; form of body for that encoding.
inline_content_type = (
    ( encoding: "json",
      body: any ) //
    ( encoding: "none" / "base64url",
      body: tstr ) //
    $$encoding-ext
  )

external_content_type = (
    url: url_value_type,
    content_hash: content_hash_type
  )

; SessionId Object
; The empty object is the placeholder for a party with no session id.
session_id_object_type = {
    ? "local": uuid_value_type,
    ? "remote": uuid_value_type
  }

session_id_parameter_type =
    session_id_object_type /
    [* ( session_id_object_type / [* session_id_object_type] )]

; Basic types and single parameter types
;_______________________________________

; RFC3339 date string
; The pattern is in two parts only to keep the lines short.  The
; first 19 characters are the date, "T" and the time.  The rest is
; the optional fraction of a second and the time zone offset.
date_type = date_and_time_type .and date_offset_type
date_and_time_type = tstr .regexp
    "[0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}[Tt][0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2}.*"
date_offset_type = tstr .regexp
    ".{19}([.][0-9]+)?([Zz]|[+-][0-9]{2}:[0-9]{2})"

; String token of the form: hash algorithm, "-", Base64Url encoded
; digest, e.g. "sha512-..."
content_hash_value_type = tstr .regexp "[a-z0-9]+-[A-Za-z0-9_-]+"

content_hash_type =
    content_hash_value_type / [* content_hash_value_type]

; Duration in seconds; MUST be non-negative
duration_type = uint / float

; Dialog Object disposition values
disposition_value_type = "no-answer" / "congestion" / "failed" /
    "busy" / "hung-up" / "voicemail-no-message" /
    $disposition-ext

; Media type of the form: type "/" subtype
media_type_value_type = tstr .regexp
    "[A-Za-z0-9!#$&^_.+-]+/[A-Za-z0-9!#$&^_.+-]+(;.*)?"

party_index_type = uint

dialog_index_type = uint

dialog_index_or_list_type = dialog_index_type / [* dialog_index_type]

attachment_index_type = uint

attachment_index_or_list_type =
    attachment_index_type / [* attachment_index_type]

session_index_type = uint

session_index_or_list_type =
    session_index_type / [* session_index_type]

analysis_index_type = uint

analysis_index_or_list_type =
    analysis_index_type / [* analysis_index_type]

; Index or indices of parties; the nested array form is used for
; multi-channel recordings with one entry per channel; null is the
; placeholder for an unused channel.
dialog_parties_type = party_index_type /
    [* ( party_index_type / [* party_index_type] / null )]

; UUID string
uuid_value_type = tstr .regexp
    "[0-9a-fA-F]{8}(-[0-9a-fA-F]{4}){3}-[0-9a-fA-F]{12}"

; HTTPS URL
url_value_type = tstr .regexp "https://.+"

; Signed Form of vCon Object
;___________________________

signed_vcon_type = {
    payload: base64url_value_type,
    signatures: [+ signature_object_type],
    * $$signed-ext
  }

; Signature Object
signature_object_type = {
    header: signed_header_object_type,
    ; present when the JWS Protected Header is not empty
    ? protected: base64url_value_type,
    signature: base64url_value_type,
    * $$signature-ext
  }

; Header Object of the signed form
; MUST include at least one of x5c or x5u.
; Other JWS header parameters MAY be present.
; Not enforced by this CDDL, as the JWS Protected Header is an
; opaque string here:
; - alg MUST be present here or in the JWS Protected Header, and
;   SHOULD be in the JWS Protected Header.
; - alg MUST NOT be "none" in the JWS Protected Header.
; - A header parameter MUST NOT be present in both the JWS
;   Protected Header and the Header Object.
signed_header_object_type = {
    ; SHOULD be "RS256"; MUST NOT be "none"
    ? alg: tstr .ne "none",
    ( ( x5c: [+ tstr],
        ? x5u: url_value_type ) //
      x5u: url_value_type ),
    ? uuid: uuid_value_type,                 ; SHOULD be provided
    * tstr => any
  }

; Encrypted Form of vCon Object
;______________________________

; Not enforced by this CDDL, as the JWE Protected Header is an
; opaque string here:
; - enc MUST be present in the JWE Protected Header or the
;   Unprotected Object.
; - alg MUST be present in the JWE Protected Header, the
;   Unprotected Object or the Header Object of each recipient.
; - A header parameter MUST NOT be present in more than one of
;   those locations.
encrypted_vcon_type = {
    ? protected: base64url_value_type,
    unprotected: unprotected_object_type,
    recipients: [+ recipient_object_type],
    iv: base64url_value_type,
    ciphertext: base64url_value_type,
    tag: base64url_value_type,
    ? aad: base64url_value_type,
    * $$encrypted-ext
  }

; Unprotected Object
; Other JWE header parameters MAY be present.
unprotected_object_type = {
    ; cty SHOULD be "application/vcon" or "application/vcon+gzip"
    cty: tstr,
    ; enc SHOULD be "A256CBC-HS512"
    ? enc: tstr,
    ? alg: tstr,
    ? uuid: uuid_value_type,                 ; SHOULD be provided
    * tstr => any
  }

; Recipient Object
; A Recipient Object with no parameters is permitted.
recipient_object_type = {
    ? header: recipient_header_object_type,
    ; present when the JWE Encrypted Key is not empty
    ? encrypted_key: base64url_value_type,
    * $$recipient-ext
  }

; Header Object of a recipient in the encrypted form
; Other JWE header parameters MAY be present.
recipient_header_object_type = {
    ? alg: tstr,                             ; SHOULD be "RSA-OAEP"
    * tstr => any
  }

; Base64Url encoded string
base64url_value_type = tstr .regexp "[A-Za-z0-9_-]+"
¶

Appendix E. vCon Extension Template

A template for writing a vCon extension Internet-Draft is provided in kramdown-rfc markdown. It contains the sections required of an extension document in Extending vCon (Section 2.5), along with JSON Schema and CDDL appendices. The template can be downloaded from: https://raw.githubusercontent.com/ietf-wg-vcon/draft-ietf-vcon-vcon-core/refs/heads/main/template-draft-vcon-xxxx-extension.md¶
